previousAction · L38–42

Purpose: Find an earlier Salesforce visit action by request key so a retry can reuse its record ID; skip this lookup in mock mode.

async function previousAction(objectName: string, visitId: string, key: string)

Inputs: objectName: string; visitId: string; key: string

Read the execution in this order

  1. Branch on !key || env.USE_MOCK_DATA.
  2. const rows = await sfRecords(`SELECT Id FROM ${objectName} WHERE Visit__c = '${escapeSoqlLiteral(visitId)}' AND Mobile_Request_Key__c = '${escapeSoqlLiteral(key)}' LIMIT 1`);
  3. Return rows[0] ? { id: rows[0].Id } : undefined.

Branch and validation expressions

  • !key || env.USE_MOCK_DATA

Calls to trace

  • sfRecords
  • escapeSoqlLiteral

Return paths

  • undefined
  • rows[0] ? { id: rows[0].Id } : undefined

ownedVisit · L44–51

Purpose: Return an employee-owned cached visit or restore it from Salesforce and cache it; never return another employee cached visit.

async function ownedVisit(id: string, employeeId: string)

Inputs: id: string; employeeId: string

Read the execution in this order

  1. const cached = visits.get(id);
  2. Branch on cached.
  3. Branch on env.USE_MOCK_DATA.
  4. const restored = await restoreVisit(String(id || ""), employeeId);
  5. Branch on restored.
  6. Return restored.

Inputs: req; res

Read the execution in this order

  6. Branch on !employee.
  7. const claims: AuthClaims = { sub: employee.email, displayName: employee.displayName, employeeId: employee.id, employeeName: employee.employeeName, salesforceUserId: employee.salesforceUserId, beatId: employee.beatId, bea
  8. Return ok( res, { token: jwt.sign(claims, env.JWT_SECRET, { expiresIn: "8h" }), user: claims, }, "Signed in successfully", ).

app.post("/api/visits", async (request, res) =>

Inputs: request; res

Read the execution in this order

  1. const req = request as AuthenticatedRequest;
  2. Branch on env.USE_MOCK_DATA.
  3. Branch on !req.body.outletId.
  4. const employeeId = escapeSoqlLiteral(req.auth!.employeeId);
  5. const outletId = escapeSoqlLiteral(String(req.body.outletId));
  6. const outlets = await sfRecords( `SELECT Id, Beat__c FROM ${objectMap.store} WHERE Id = '${outletId}' AND Id IN (${allocatedOutletIds(employeeId)}) LIMIT 1`, );
  7. Branch on !outlets.length.
  8. const plans = await sfRecords( `SELECT Id FROM ${objectMap.journeyPlan} WHERE Beat__c = '${outlets[0].Beat__c}' AND Status__c = 'Approved' ORDER BY Planned_Date__c DESC LIMIT 1`, );
  9. Branch on !plans.length.
  10. const created: any = await salesforce.create(objectMap.visit, { Name: req.body.name || `Mobile Visit ${new Date().toLocaleDateString("en-IN")}`, Retailer__c: req.body.outletId, Permanent_Journey_Plan__c: plans[0].Id, Emp
  11. ok(res, { id: created.id }, "Visit created in Salesforce");
